________ test_TC_4209_joined_segments_are_the_text_without_its_markers ________

    def test_TC_4209_joined_segments_are_the_text_without_its_markers():
        """TC-4209 (LLR-4201.9, A-5/SEC-9). Over the derived set (10 pieces × up to 4 =
        11 110 inputs): 0 exceptions; the joined segment text equals the reference scanner's
        marker-free text for 100 %; every emitted tone is in the closed set
        {mut, soon, over, green, orange, blue, violet} (the `hl-<tone>` class names of
        notes_html come from it). RED on base: the colour markers stay in the text. NC: drop
        the text between two spans \u2192 RED."""
        assert len(DERIVED) == 11110
        errors, wrong, bad_tone = [], [], []
        for t in DERIVED:
            try:
                segs = highlight_segments(t)
            except Exception as exc:                   # noqa: BLE001 — counted, not hidden
                errors.append((t, repr(exc)))
                continue
            if "".join(s for s, _ in segs) != ref_visible(t):
                wrong.append(t)
            bad_tone += [(t, tone) for _s, tone in segs if tone not in CLOSED_TONES]
        assert not errors, errors[:5]
>       assert not wrong, [(t, highlight_segments(t), ref_visible(t)) for t in wrong[:5]]
E       AssertionError: [('::b::', [('::b::', 'mut')], ''), ('::r::', [('::r::', 'mut')], ''), ('ab::b::', [('ab::b::', 'mut')], 'ab'), ('ab::r::', [('ab::r::', 'mut')], 'ab'), (' ::b::', [(' ::b::', 'mut')], ' ')]
E       assert not ['::b::', '::r::', 'ab::b::', 'ab::r::', ' ::b::', ' ::r::', ...]

    def test_TC_4224_the_decks_tone_variables_read_on_their_themes_background(tmp_path):
        """TC-4224 (LLR-4206.5): each of the deck's six tone variables (soon, over, green,
        orange, blue, violet) has contrast >= 4.5:1 on its theme's `--bg`, dark and light,
        12/12. NC: light `--blue:#60a5fa` on `#ffffff` (2.5:1) \u2192 RED. RED on base: three of
        the six variables are absent."""
        blocks = _blocks(report_deck.render(_extract(_board(tmp_path, "x"))))
        bad, missing = [], []
        for theme in ("dark", "light"):
            bg = blocks[theme].get("bg")
            assert bg, (theme, blocks[theme])
            for tone in ("soon", "over", "green", "orange", "blue", "violet"):
                v = blocks[theme].get(tone)
                if v is None:
                    missing.append((theme, tone))
                elif contrast(v, bg) < 4.5:
                    bad.append((theme, tone, v, round(contrast(v, bg), 2)))
>       assert not missing, missing
E       AssertionError: [('dark', 'orange'), ('dark', 'blue'), ('dark', 'violet'), ('light', 'orange'), ('light', 'blue'), ('light', 'violet')]
E       assert not [('dark', 'orange'), ('dark', 'blue'), ('dark', 'violet'), ('light', 'orange'), ('light', 'blue'), ('light', 'violet')]

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("size", [(118, 30), (80, 24)], ids=lambda s: f"{s[0]}x{s[1]}")
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("state", STATES, ids=lambda s: "none" if s is None else CODES[s])
    async def test_TC_4239_the_label_shows_the_pen_and_every_key_unclipped(tmp_path, size, state):
        """TC-4239 (LLR-4203.9, LLR-4202.10): in each of the 7 pen states, at 118x30 and
        80x24, the label's VISIBLE cells hold the state (`pen: none`, or a chip naming the pen
        on its tone), the keys `1`..`6` each in its tone, `0 off` and `alt+c` — the whole
        label from `Notes` to `alt+c`, 0 clipped glyphs (14/14). NC: base label \u2192 RED."""
        app = _app(tmp_path, "")
        async with app.run_test(size=size) as pilot:
            scr, ta = await _editor(app, pilot)
            if state is not None:
                await pilot.press(f"alt+{state + 1}")
                await pilot.pause()
            cells = _label_cells(app, scr)
            vis = _text(cells)
            body = vis.strip()

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("notes, cursor, key, notice", [
        ("::r:: ab ::", (0, 7), "alt+5", NOTICE_PEN),      # inside a colour span (LLR-4203.12)
        ("::b:: abc ::", (0, 7), "alt+5", NOTICE_PEN),     # same code: no "continue in span"
        ("==late==", (0, 1), "alt+5", NOTICE_PEN),         # inside a legacy span's opener
        ("b:: x", (0, 0), "alt+1", NOTICE_N4),             # `b::` would become an opener (LLR-4203.17)
        ("C::b", (0, 4), "alt+1", NOTICE_N4),              # would show `Cr  ::`
        ("note y::", (0, 5), "alt+1", NOTICE_N4),          # the pair's `::` + `y::` would open
    ])
    async def test_TC_4252_a_pen_refuses_where_it_would_change_written_text(
            tmp_path, notes, cursor, key, notice):
        """TC-4252 (LLR-4203.12, LLR-4203.17; P-M1-3, SEC-2): with no pen on, `alt+<n>` inside
        a span, or where the pair would fuse with the neighbouring text, inserts nothing,
        keeps the pen off and shows ONE notice. NC: insert anyway \u2192 RED. RED on base: no
        notice."""
        app = _app(tmp_path, notes)
        async with app.run_test(size=SIZE) as pilot:
            scr, ta = await _editor(app, pilot, cursor)
            before = _ids(app)
            await pilot.press(key)
            await pilot.pause()
            assert ta.text == notes
            assert ta.cursor_location == cursor
>           assert _new_notices(app, before) == [notice]

    async def test_AT_4201_a_pen_written_note_is_saved_and_shown_blue(tmp_path):
        """AT-4201 (HLR-4203, HLR-4204, US-4201). Open the task with `e`, reach the notes with
        `tab`, then the bytes Windows Terminal sends: `ESC 5`, `ask`, `ESC 0`, ` now`,
        `ESC s` (alt+s). The saved board holds `::b:: ask :: now`; the details screen
        (`enter`, the app's binding for TaskDetails) shows `ask` in `#60a5fa` and ` now` not
        blue; the editor's field showed `ask` blue while it was open. RED on base: the terminal's
        `ESC 5` types `\u221e`."""
        app = _app(tmp_path, "")
        async with app.run_test(size=SIZE) as pilot:
            await pilot.pause()
            app.selected_task_id = app.board.tasks[0].id
            await pilot.press("e")
            for _ in range(3):
                await pilot.pause()
            scr = app.screen
            assert isinstance(scr, TaskModal)
            for _ in range(25):
                if app.focused is not None and app.focused.id == "f-notes":
                    break
                await pilot.press("tab")
            ta = scr.query_one("#f-notes", TextArea)
            assert app.focused is ta
            await _terminal(app, pilot, "\x1b5", "a", "s", "k")

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("line, a, b, code, new, col", RECOLOUR,

    def test_TC_4263_recolour_rewrites_the_target_or_its_span(line, a, b, code, new, col):

        changes nothing. `col` is the column just after the written closer. NC: unspaced form,

    async def test_TC_4270_alt_c_refuses_with_one_notice_and_no_palette(tmp_path, notes, kw, notice):
        """TC-4270, TC-4271, TC-4272, TC-4280, TC-4281 (LLR-4205.10/.11/.12): a multi-line
        selection, a target over a colour edge, or nothing to colour \u2192 no palette, ONE
        notice saying why, the notes byte-equal. NC: open anyway \u2192 RED."""

tests\test_colour_palette.py:439: AssertionError
__ test_TC_4273_the_palette_box_is_on_screen_next_to_the_target[first-80x24] __

tmp_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/jjgh8/AppData/Local/Temp/pytest-of-jjgh8/pytest-31550/test_TC_4273_the_palette_box_i0')
size = (80, 24), where = 'first'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("size", [(80, 24), (118, 30)], ids=lambda s: f"{s[0]}x{s[1]}")
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("where", ["first", "last"])
    async def test_TC_4273_the_palette_box_is_on_screen_next_to_the_target(tmp_path, size, where):
        """TC-4273 (LLR-4205.13): at 80x24 and 118x30, for a target on the notes field's first
        and last visible row, the box lies wholly on screen and its top row is the anchor row
        + 1, or its bottom row the anchor row \u2212 1 (4/4). NC: no clamping \u2192 RED at 80x24 last
        row."""
        notes = "plan" if where == "first" else "\n".join(f"line {n}" for n in range(30))
        app = _app(tmp_path, notes)
        async with app.run_test(size=size) as pilot:
            scr, ta = await _editor(app, pilot, (0, 0))
            if where == "first":
                ta.move_cursor((0, 4))
            else:
                ta.scroll_to(y=0, animate=False)
                r = ta.content_region.height - 1
                ta.move_cursor((r, 6))
            for _ in range(2):
                await pilot.pause()
            anchor = ta.cursor_screen_offset
            if where == "last":
                assert anchor.y == ta.content_region.y + ta.content_region.height - 1
            await _alt_c(pilot)
>           assert _on_palette(app), f"no palette: the screen is {type(app.screen).__name__}"
E           AssertionError: no palette: the screen is TaskModal
E           assert False
E            +  where False = _on_palette(TaskboardApp(title='taskboard', classes={'-dark-mode'}, pseudo_classes={'focus', 'dark'}))
